   172→
   173→<script setup lang="ts">
   174→import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
   175→
   176→useHead({
   177→  title: 'OhMyDoc — Free Resume Formatter | Paste → Format → PDF',
   178→})
   179→
   180→const placeholder = `Paste your resume or cover letter here.
   181→
   182→Example:
   183→Jane Smith
   184→jane@email.com · Toronto, ON
   185→
   186→Software Engineer with 8 years...`
   187→
   188→const textContent = ref('')
   189→const formattedXml = ref('')
   190→const isFormatting = ref(false)
   191→const formatError = ref('')
   192→const documentType = ref('')
   193→const divergence = ref(0)
   194→const validationMessage = ref('')
   195→const selectedDocType = ref<'resume' | 'letter'>('resume')
   196→const viewMode = ref<'text' | 'xml'>('text')
   197→
   198→const formatButtonLabel = computed(() =>
   199→  selectedDocType.value === 'letter' ? 'Format My Letter' : 'Format My Resume',
   200→)
   201→
   202→const showDivergenceWarning = computed(() => formattedXml.value && divergence.value > 0.05)
   203→
   204→function handlePrint() {
   205→  window.print()
   206→}
   207→
   208→async function loadSample(type: 'resume' | 'letter') {
   209→  const filename = type === 'resume' ? 'resume-sample.txt' : 'letter-sample.txt'
   210→  const response = await fetch(`/samples/${filename}`)
   211→  const text = await response.text()
   212→  textContent.value = text
   213→  selectedDocType.value = type
   214→  formatDocument()
   215→}
   216→
   217→function reformat() {
   218→  viewMode.value = 'text'
   219→  formatDocument()
   220→}
   221→
   222→async function formatDocument() {
   223→  validationMessage.value = ''
   224→
   225→  if (!textContent.value.trim()) return
   226→
   227→  if (textContent.value.trim().length < 50) {
   228→    validationMessage.value = 'Please paste at least 50 characters of content to format.'
   229→    return
   230→  }
   231→
   232→  if (isFormatting.value) return
   233→
   234→  isFormatting.value = true
   235→  formatError.value = ''
   236→  formattedXml.value = ''
   237→  documentType.value = ''
   238→  divergence.value = 0
   239→
   240→  try {
   241→    const result = await $fetch<{ xml: string, divergence: number, documentType: string }>('/api/format', {
   242→      method: 'POST',
   243→      body: { text: textContent.value, docType: selectedDocType.value },
   244→    })
   245→    formattedXml.value = result.xml
   246→    documentType.value = result.documentType
   247→    divergence.value = result.divergence
   248→  }
   249→  catch (err: unknown) {
   250→    const raw = (err as { data?: { rawResponse?: string } })?.data?.rawResponse
   251→    if (raw) {
   252→      console.error('[OhMyDoc] Raw AI response (malformed XML):', raw)
   253→    }
   254→    const message = (err as { data?: { message?: string } })?.data?.message
   255→    formatError.value = message || 'Something went wrong. Please try again.'
   256→  }
   257→  finally {
   258→    isFormatting.value = false
   259→  }
   260→}
   261→</script>
